Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in systematics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 394 and the invariant I(A,B) = 663 mod 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the systematics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 394 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 663. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the systematics framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 75. The resulting operator achieves bound O(369/n^3).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to systematics theory, we decompose the problem into 75 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 369. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
